### [307] [Context-Aware Synthesis of Optimization Pipelines for Warehouse Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26852)
*Janik Bischoff,Anne Meyer,Uta Mohring,Fabian Dunke,Maximilian Barlang,Özge Nur Subas,Hadi Kutabi,Stefan Nickel,Kai Furmans*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CASOP是一个上下文感知的优化流水线合成框架，能够根据给定的仓库场景，从模块化算法库中自动组合出所有有效的订单履行求解流水线，并对其进行评估，以支持研究人员和从业者设计、选择和部署高性能的仓库运营算法方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动拣货仓库的订单履行涉及物品分配、订单分批与拣货路径规划等多个相互关联的决策。现有研究主要评估孤立子问题或特定场景下的固定子问题组合的算法，缺乏一种通用机制来确定适用的算法配置、将其组合为有效求解流水线并评估其性能。

Method: 提出CASOP框架，包含五个核心组件：（1）模块化算法仓库，涵盖常见订单履行问题的算法；（2）语义数据卡与算法卡，描述仓库上下文和算法需求；（3）将订单履行问题结构化为相关子问题的分类体系；（4）流水线合成器，根据给定仓库上下文识别适用算法并组合所有有效优化流水线；（5）流水线评估器，对所有生成的流水线进行性能评估。

Result: 在覆盖四类问题的7个基准实例集上验证了框架，共生成了1,063,044条有效流水线。软件已开源，可通过GitHub获取。

Conclusion: CASOP框架填补了仓库优化中缺乏通用算法配置与流水线合成机制的空白，为不同仓库场景自动生成并筛选高效求解方案提供了系统化工具，具有显著的学术价值与实践应用前景。

Abstract: Order fulfillment in manual picker-to-goods warehouses involves interconnected decisions such as item assignment, order batching, and picker routing. While integrated models capture interactions between these decisions, practical warehouse systems often require decomposed approaches due to organizational boundaries, differing responsibilities, or limited data availability. Existing studies primarily evaluate algorithms for isolated subproblems or fixed subproblem combinations for specific warehouse settings, but lack a general mechanism to determine applicable algorithm configurations, compose them into valid solution pipelines, and assess their performance.
  With Context-Aware Synthesis of Optimization Pipelines (CASOP), we propose a framework for constructing and evaluating context-specific optimization pipelines and apply these to order fulfillment. The framework comprises: (1) a modular repository of algorithms for common order fulfillment problems; (2) semantic data and algorithm cards to describe warehouse context and algorithm requirements; (3) a taxonomy that structures order fulfillment problems into relevant subproblems; (4) a pipeline synthesizer that identifies applicable algorithms for a given warehouse context and composes all valid optimization pipelines; and (5) a pipeline evaluator that assesses all resulting pipelines. We demonstrate the framework on 7 benchmark instance sets covering four problem classes, resulting in 1,063,044 valid pipelines. The framework supports researchers and practitioners in designing, automatically synthesizing, and selecting valid, high-performing algorithmic pipelines for warehouse operations. The software is open-source and available at https://github.com/kit-dsm/ware_ops_pipes and https://github.com/kit-dsm/ware_ops_algos.
  Keywords: Warehouse optimization, Algorithm selection, Pipeline synthesis, Order fulfillment

</details>


### [308] [LCAi: Life Cycle Assessment with big data fusion and retrieval-augmented generation-assisted interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26857)
*Georgios Tsironis,Juan D. Medrano-Garcia,Gonzalo Guillen-Gosalbez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LCA解释阶段的多视角检索增强生成框架（Perspective-Conditioned RAG），通过融合学术、产业、公共话语和欧盟资助等多源数据，将量化的环境改善机会转化为不确定性下的可操作战略路径，并在意大利苹果生产的氢能替代柴油案例中进行了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCA解释阶段缺乏结构化机制，难以将识别出的环境热点改善机会转化为技术、社会和政策不确定性下的可操作战略路径，传统LCA在支持实施导向决策方面存在明显不足。

Method: 提出三步骤的多视角检索增强生成框架：（1）场景锚定：定义系统边界和脱碳目标；（2）多视角微观查询：基于学术、产业、公共话语、欧盟资助四个视角进行约束性检索；（3）中性合成：仅整合账本存储输出，不再进一步检索。框架在意大利苹果生产设施中，以GPT-5 nano为推理模型，演示了氢能替代柴油的用例。

Result: 结构化检索与约束合成有效降低了幻觉风险，同时保留了跨领域多样性，实现了从影响结果到战略路径的规范化转化。概念验证表明，AI辅助的循证解释可支持超越传统LCA研究的实施导向决策。

Conclusion: 该框架为LCA解释阶段引入了更严谨的多视角证据整合机制，为先进AI工具在LCA研究（尤其是可规模化部署技术）中的应用开辟了新途径，推动了LCA从评估走向实施决策的范式拓展。

Abstract: The interpretation phase of life cycle assessment often lacks structured mechanisms for translating quantified improvement opportunities addressing environmental hotspots into actionable strategic pathways under technological, social, and policy uncertainty. To overcome this limitation, this study introduces a perspective-conditioned retrieval-augmented generation framework for LCA interpretation, where a multi-perspective retrieval and controlled synthesis is incorporated in the artificial intelligence (AI)-assisted LCA. To operationalise large language models in LCA interpretation, a perspective fusion RAG architecture was developed, covering academic, industry, public discourse, and European union (EU) funding datasets. Our approach comprises three steps: (1) a scenario anchor defining system boundaries and decarbonization targets, (2) a set of perspective-specific micro-queries with constrained retrieval, and (3) a neutral synthesis step integrating only ledger-stored outputs without further retrieval. The framework is demonstrated through a hydrogen-enabled diesel reduction use case in an Italian apple production facility using GPT-5 nano as the reasoning model. Overall, the structured retrieval and constrained synthesis are designed to mitigate the risk of hallucination while preserving cross-domain diversity. The approach presented can support more disciplined translation of impact results into strategic pathways and opens up new avenues for the use of advanced AI tools in LCA studies, particularly those focused on technologies that could be deployed at scale. This proof-of-concept demonstrates how AI-assisted, evidence-grounded interpretation can support implementation-oriented decision-making beyond conventional LCA studies.

</details>


### [309] [AgentX: Towards Agent-Driven Self-Iteration of Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26859)
*Changxin Lao,Fei Pan,Guozhuang Ma,Han Li,Huihuang Lin,Jijun Shi,Kangzhi Zhao,Kun Gai,Mo Zhou,Qinqin Zhou,Quan Chen,Ruochen Yang,Shifu Bie,Shuang Yang,Shuo Yang,Wenhao Li,Wentao Xie,Xiao Lv,Xuming Wang,Yijun Wang,Yiming Chen,Yusheng Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Zibo Zhao,Zijie Zhuang,Baoning Xia,Chao Liu,Chaoyi Ma,Chubo He,Dawei Cong,Feng Jiang,Gang Wang,Guilin Xia,Hanwen Xu,Jiahong Xie,Jiahui Qiao,Jian Liang,Jiangfan Yue,Jing Wang,Jinghan Yang,Jinghui Jia,Kan Qin,Lei Wang,Ming Li,Peilin Song,Pengbo Xu,Qiang Luo,Ruiming Tang,Shiyang Liu,Shuxian Jin,Tao Wang,Tao Zhang,Xiang Gao,Xianghan Li,Yingsong Luo,Yiwen Ning,Yongcheng Liu,Yuan Guo,Zhaojie Liu,Zhenkai Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentX 是一个已部署于生产环境的多智能体系统，将推荐算法的"创意到上线"循环全流程自动化，涵盖头脑风暴、代码开发、在线评估与智能体自身进化四个闭环阶段，实现了自我改进的推荐实验研发引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推荐算法的迭代流程存在结构性执行瓶颈：从假设生成、代码修改、A/B实验到结果归因，整个链条依赖人工工程师，导致创新速度随人力线性增长，而非随证据、算力和实验知识的积累实现复合增长。

Method: AgentX 由四个紧密耦合的闭环阶段构成：(1) Brainstorm Agent——综合历史实验证据、系统架构、数据分析和外部研究，生成可执行的排序提案；(2) Developing Agent——通过代码仓库感知的生成与多维可靠性验证，将提案转化为生产就绪代码；(3) Evaluation Agent——以护栏否决（guardrail-vetoed）机制进行安全的在线A/B发布，并将成功与失败均转化为结构化知识资产；(4) Harness Evolution 层（SGPO）——将执行轨迹蒸馏为语义梯度更新，持续优化各智能体自身，实现系统自我改进。

Result: AgentX 实现了远超人工工作流的实验生成、实施与评估的规模和速度，并已实际部署于生产环境中。

Conclusion: AgentX 通过将推荐实验全流程闭环自动化并引入智能体自我进化机制（SGPO），从根本上重构了推荐算法研发的生产函数，使创新能够随知识与算力的积累而复合增长，突破了人工工作流的结构性瓶颈。

Abstract: Recommendation algorithm iteration is moving from an artisanal, engineer-bound process toward an industrialized research loop, but this transition remains blocked by a structural execution bottleneck: the idea-to-launch cycle still depends on human engineers to generate hypotheses, modify production code, launch A/B experiments, and attribute online results. Innovation therefore scales linearly with headcount rather than compounding with evidence, compute, and accumulated experimental knowledge. We present AgentX, a production-deployed multi-agent system that fundamentally restructures this production function. AgentX operates as a self-evolving development engine: it autonomously generates, implements, evaluates, and learns from recommendation experiments at a scale and pace that no manual workflow can sustain.
  The system orchestrates four tightly coupled stages in a closed loop. A Brainstorm Agent synthesizes evidence from historical experiments, system architecture, data analysis, and external research into ranked, executable proposals. A Developing Agent translates each proposal into production-ready code through repository-grounded generation and multi-dimensional reliability verification. An Evaluation Agent conducts safe online rollout with guardrail-vetoed A/B judgment, converting both successes and failures into structured knowledge assets. A Harness Evolution layer (SGPO) then distills execution trajectories into semantic-gradient updates that continuously sharpen the agents themselves -- making the system not merely automated, but self-improving.

</details>


### [310] [TAVR-VLM: Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding for Hallucination-Resistant Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26874)
*Zhixiang Lu,Xiwei Liu,Sifan Song,Changkai Ji,Anh Nguyen,Jionglong Su,Imran Razzak,Jinfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TAVR-VLM 框架提出风险条件因果归因注意力机制（R-CGA），通过“风险→区域→词汇”的结构化归因路径压缩多模态特征，在 1,482 例患者的 M³TAVR 数据集上实现 AUROC 0.896、CIDEr 0.936，并将幻觉率降至 8.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAVR 规划需要精细的多模态推理，但现有 MLLM 在此高风险领域存在严重的诊断幻觉问题——生成的文本缺乏解剖学依据，亟需一种具备解剖学归因能力的可信框架。

Method: 提出 TAVR-VLM 框架，核心为 R-CGA 机制：(1) 将多模态输入压缩为“因果风险瓶颈”，提炼密集视觉特征为全局风险掩码；(2) 在自回归生成中，通过支持投影因果一致性约束，将 token 级生成限定在风险定义的支持掩码内，构建“风险→区域→词汇”的结构化归因通路。

Result: 在 1,482 例患者的多模态 TAVR 数据集 M³TAVR 上，TAVR-VLM 达到 AUROC 0.896、CIDEr 0.936，幻觉率大幅降至 8.1%，全面达到最优水平。

Conclusion: TAVR-VLM 通过解剖学归因机制有效抑制诊断幻觉，显著提升了循证手术 AI 的可解释性与可靠性，为高风险临床场景中 MLLM 的安全部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Transcatheter Aortic Valve Replacement (TAVR) planning requires meticulous multimodal reasoning. However, adapting Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to this high-stakes domain is severely impeded by diagnostic hallucinations, where generated text lacks anatomical grounding. To address this, TAVR-VLM is introduced: a novel framework featuring Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding Attention (R-CGA) that instantiates a model-internal ``Risk $\rightarrow$ Region $\rightarrow$ Word'' structural grounding pathway. R-CGA compresses multimodal inputs into a causal risk bottleneck, purifying dense visual features into a global risk mask. During autoregressive generation, a support-projected causal consistency objective constrains token-level grounding within the risk-defined support mask. Evaluated on $\text{M}^3\text{TAVR}$, a comprehensive 1,482-patient cohort, TAVR-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art. It achieves an AUROC of 0.896, boosts CIDEr to 0.936, and drastically reduces the hallucination rate to 8.1\%, thereby improving interpretability for evidence-based surgical AI.

</details>


### [311] [A Pipeline for Generating Longitudinal Synthetic Clinical Notes Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26879)
*William Poulett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套合成临床笔记的生成管线与数据集，通过结构化患者生成、半结构化病程模拟及大语言模型非结构化文本生成，生成纵向一致的合成病历，并辅以验证与增强机制提升真实性与多样性，最终发布含70名患者、每人20-50份笔记的数据集，用于临床AI系统的开发与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗保健领域，真实临床文档因涉及患者隐私而难以获取，限制了临床AI工具的开发与评估。现有的合成数据方法难以同时保证纵向记录的一致性、书写风格的多样性和临床细节的逼真度，因此需要一种能够兼顾隐私保护与数据实用性的合成临床笔记生成方案。

Method: 采用模块化管线设计，包含三个核心环节：(1) 结构化患者生成——合成患者人口统计学与基础临床特征；(2) 半结构化患者旅程模拟——构建贯穿住院全过程的临床事件时间线；(3) 基于大语言模型的非结构化临床笔记生成——将结构化事件转化为自然语言笔记。此外，引入基于LLM的验证机制和增强步骤，以提升生成笔记的忠实性（faithfulness）、真实感和多样性。

Result: 发布了一个包含70名合成患者的数据集，每名患者关联20至50份覆盖完整住院旅程的临床笔记。数据集提供多个验证级别，用户可根据自身用例在真实性与可扩展性之间进行权衡。

Conclusion: 该合成临床笔记数据集可在不依赖真实患者数据的前提下，支持临床AI系统的开发、测试与评估，涵盖摘要工具、编码模型和决策支持系统等多种应用场景，有效规避了隐私风险。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly used to enable the development and evaluation of AI systems in domains where access to real-world data is restricted. In healthcare, clinical documentation presents particular challenges due to its sensitivity. This work introduces a synthetic clinical notes pipeline and dataset designed to support the development of clinical AI tools while avoiding the privacy risks associated with real patient data. The dataset is generated using a modular pipeline that combines structured patient generation, semi-structured patient journey simulation, and unstructured clinical note generation using large language models. The pipeline is designed to prioritise internal consistency across longitudinal patient records, while also capturing variation in writing style, note structure, and clinical detail. Additional mechanisms, including LLM-based validation and augmentation steps, are used to improve faithfulness, realism, and diversity of the generated notes. We release a dataset of 70 synthetic patients, each associated with 20-50 clinical notes spanning a full hospital journey. The dataset is provided at multiple levels of validation, enabling users to balance realism and scalability depending on their use case. This dataset supports the development, testing, and evaluation of clinical AI systems, including summarisation tools, coding models, and decision support systems, without reliance on real patient data.

</details>


### [312] [Generative Retrieval via Diffusion Transformer with Metric-Ordered Sequence Training and Hybrid-Policy Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26899)
*Chenghao Liu,Yu Zhang,Zhongtao Jiang,Kun Xu,Zhenwei An,Renzhi Wang,Zhao Wang,Jiachen Zhang,Yuxiao Zhang,Kun Xu,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"模式保持的属性检索"任务：给定一个表达细粒度模式的种子集，需要检索同时满足目标属性且保持该模式的物品。提出MO-DiT+HPPO框架，通过度量有序预训练和混合偏好优化，在不牺牲模式纯度的情况下提升属性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的检索通常只追求与查询的高相似度，但在实际生产场景中，需要的是：给定一个体现细粒度模式的种子集，找到既满足目标属性又不偏离该模式的物品。直接平均种子向量会陷入低属性区域，而全局属性检索又会漂移到无关模式，两者存在根本性张力。

Method: 提出MO-DiT+HPPO两阶段框架：(1) 原始序列预训练；(2) 多领域度量有序延续预训练——将稀疏在线检索标签转化为从低到高预测属性密度的"模式内轨迹"，教会模型跨领域的度量改进方向；(3) 尾部质心微调；(4) HPPO混合偏好优化——用在线交集度量标注混合候选池，通过参考锚定偏好优化对齐生成查询分布与真实在线目标，并引入Pareto对过滤器仅保留不降低同模式纯度的优胜对。

Result: 在四个属性领域、物品和模式留出协议下，度量有序DiT相比预训练生成检索器提升了交集度量，HPPO进一步改善。八个领域-划分单元中七个取得显著收益，最困难划分上持平。通过度量预测器验证、顺序消融、CPT/SFT对比和候选策略消融实验验证了收益来源。

Conclusion: MO-DiT+HPPO框架有效解决了模式保持与属性检索之间的张力，度量有序训练和HPPO是两个关键贡献，在多个领域和划分协议下均展现出稳健的性能提升。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval ranks items by their similarity to a query in a shared vector space and usually aims to return the highest-scoring items. In many production settings this is not what is wanted: given a seed set that expresses a fine-grained pattern, one needs more items that both satisfy a target attribute and stay within that pattern. We formalize this as pattern-preserving attribute retrieval. The two goals pull against each other: averaging the seeds preserves the pattern but stays in a low-attribute region, while global attribute retrieval drifts to unrelated patterns. We approach the task with continuous generative retrieval, where a model reads a sequence of item embeddings and generates query embeddings for nearest-neighbor search. We propose MO-DiT+HPPO, a staged framework with raw-sequence pretraining, multi-domain metric-ordered continuation pretraining, tail-centroid fine-tuning, and HPPO. Metric-ordered training turns sparse online retrieval labels into in-pattern trajectories ordered from low to high predicted attribute density, teaching one model the metric-improvement direction across domains. HPPO aligns the generated query distribution with the true online objective by labeling a hybrid candidate pool with the online intersection metric and applying reference-anchored preference optimization. A Pareto pair filter keeps only winner pairs that do not lower same-pattern purity, raising the attribute metric without sacrificing the pattern. Across four attribute domains under item- and pattern-holdout protocols, metric-ordered DiT improves the intersection metric over a pretrained generative retriever, and HPPO improves it further, with significant gains on seven of eight domain-split cells and a marginal tie on the hardest split. Metric-predictor validation, order ablations, CPT/SFT comparisons, and a candidate-policy ablation show where the gains come from.

</details>


### [313] [Learning to Recover Task Experts from a Multi-Task Merged Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26902)
*Jinwook Jung,Taegyu Kim,Kumju Jo,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTeX 提出一种从单一合并checkpoint中恢复任务专家性能的框架。它将参数干扰建模为仿射变换（近似为加性偏移），并通过预测这些偏移来消除干扰。同时引入基于SVD子空间签名的无路由器任务识别器，在推理时选择最优专家。该方法在视觉和NLP领域恢复超过95%的独立专家性能，并能对OOD任务实现专家知识的自适应插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态多任务模型合并存在参数干扰问题，而现有动态合并方法需要在推理时存储和加载冗余的专家组件，成本高昂。作者希望从任务专家视角出发，将参数干扰形式化建模，从而在仅保留单一合并checkpoint的前提下恢复各任务专家性能，降低成本。

Method: 1. 将合并过程中的参数干扰建模为仿射变换，并近似为加性偏移（additive offsets）；2. 提出 ReTeX 框架，通过预测这些偏移来消除参数干扰，从单一合并checkpoint中恢复任务专家性能；3. 设计基于SVD子空间签名的任务识别器：离线计算各任务子空间签名，推理时选择使输入投影残差最小的任务子空间，无需额外路由器。

Result: 在视觉和NLP两个领域，ReTeX恢复超过95%的独立专家性能；在未见任务上显著提升泛化能力；参数偏移预测在OOD任务上涌现出对已知专家知识的自适应插值能力。

Conclusion: ReTeX 通过将参数干扰建模为加性偏移并进行预测恢复，配合基于SVD子空间签名的任务识别，实现了高效、低成本的多任务模型合并与专家性能恢复，并展现出对未见面任务的涌现泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging aims to consolidate several task-specific experts into a unified model, yet static merging consistently suffers from parameter interference. While dynamic merging models aim to bridge this gap, many works rely on the costly storage and loading of redundant expert components at inference. In this work, from the perspective of task expert, we view parameter interference as parameter perturbation introduced to each expert during merging process. We show that such parameter perturbations can be modeled as affine transformation, which can be approximated as additive offsets. Motivated by these, we propose Recover Task eXpert (ReTeX), a framework that predicts those offsets, in order to undo parameter interference and recover task-expert performance from a single merged checkpoint. To recover the appropriate expert when task identity is unknown, we introduce a router-free task identifier based on SVD subspace signatures computed offline before inference. At inference, the identifier selects the task whose subspace yields the smallest projection residual for a given input. As a result, ReTeX recovers over 95% of individual-expert performance in both vision and NLP domains, while significantly improving generalization to unseen tasks. Crucially, we also show that the parameter offset prediction leads to emergent adaptive interpolation of expert knowledge for out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. ReTeX adaptively interpolates seen expert knowledge to handle unseen tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/BAIKLAB/ReTeX

</details>


### [314] [Diagnosing Task Insensitivity in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26918)
*Jingyu Liu,Xiaopeng Wu,Kehan Chen,Chuan Yu,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型作为长程智能体时，其分布外（OOD）泛化能力薄弱，根源在于"任务不敏感"——模型倾向于依赖训练中学到的模式，而忽略当前任务指令。作者提出了一种轻量级对比正则化方法 Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization 来强制动作依赖于任务指令，从而改善任务敏感性和 OOD 泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大语言模型智能体在 OOD 场景下泛化失败的关键原因：模型对任务指令不敏感（task insensitivity）。具体表现为，即使任务描述被语义破坏或被替换为相似但不同的任务，模型仍会延续原始训练任务中学到的行为模式，而不是根据当前指令调整动作。作者进一步发现这与训练过程中注意力从任务 token 漂移到局部观测 token 的"捷径偏好"现象有关。

Method: 作者提出了一种名为 Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization 的轻量级对比正则化方法。该方法在负对数似然（NLL）损失基础上，显式地加入对比正则项，通过对任务描述进行扰动（perturbation），迫使模型的动作预测依赖于正确的任务指令，而非训练中习得的统计捷径，从而增强模型对任务语义的敏感性。

Result: 在广泛的评估中，该方法有效提升了模型的任务敏感性（task sensitivity）和分布外泛化能力，同时使模型对任务 token 的注意力更加稳定，缓解了注意力向局部观测漂移的问题。

Conclusion: 任务不敏感是大语言模型智能体 OOD 泛化失败的重要根源，其背后存在优化层面的"捷径偏好"。通过 Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization 这一轻量级对比正则化手段，可以有效地纠正该问题，使模型更关注任务指令本身，从而提升在未见任务上的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models can serve as capable long-horizon agents, but their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains weak. We identify a key source of this failure as task insensitivity: when faced with similar but distinct tasks, models might apply patterns learned during training and fail to solve the task at hand. We show that models often continue with actions aligned with the original task even when the instruction is semantically corrupted and cannot be directly answered. We further find that, when we replace the task description in a trained prompt with another similar but distinct task, the model may still output the same action. This behavior is accompanied by a consistent training-time attention drift away from task tokens and toward local observations, suggesting an optimization bias toward shortcuts. To mitigate this problem, we propose Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization, a lightweight contrastive regularizer that explicitly encourages action dependence on the task instruction. Extensive evaluations show that our intervention improves task sensitivity and OOD generalization while preserving more stable attention to task tokens.

</details>


### [315] [Where Do CoT Training Gains Land in LLM based Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26935)
*Jingyu Liu,Zhiwen Wang,Yuxin Jing,Huanyu Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了思维链（CoT）训练究竟提升了什么：模型是通过生成推理来改变行动，还是直接根据提示预测行动？研究发现，CoT训练主要提升了"提示行动"（无需CoT的预测）的质量，而非扩大CoT推理的相对优势，且后期检查点更少根据CoT修改行动。基于此，作者提出选择性掩码行动令牌监督，提升了域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，语言模型中的口头化思维链（CoT）并非总是忠实的，可能只是事后合理化（即模型在推理前已知道答案）。这引发了一个核心问题：CoT训练究竟在提升什么能力？是模型通过生成推理来改变行动的能力，还是仅仅提升直接从提示预测行动的能力？

Method: 作者通过对比"提示行动"（不用CoT直接预测行动）与"CoT行动"（使用CoT预测行动）来研究上述问题。具体做法包括：(1) 在不同检查点比较两类行动的质量变化；(2) 分析模型在环境交互中CoT行动相对于提示行动的优势是否随训练扩大；(3) 检查后期检查点是否更少根据CoT修改行动；(4) 基于发现的模式，在部分训练样本上选择性掩码行动令牌的监督信号，以验证该干预的效果。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 随训练推进，提示行动质量显著提升；(2) 在与环境交互时，CoT行动相对于提示行动的优势保持稳定，CoT训练并未扩大这一优势；(3) 后期检查点更不倾向于根据CoT修改行动，表明模型越来越依赖提示本身；(4) 选择性掩码行动令牌监督可改善模型的域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoT训练的主要效果在于提升模型从提示中直接预测行动的能力（即"提示行动"），而非增强通过推理修改行动的能力。这一发现挑战了传统对CoT训练作用的理解，并表明通过选择性掩码行动监督可以减少模型对提示的过度依赖，从而提升泛化性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely used in language-model agents, but prior work has shown that verbalized CoT is not always faithful and may instead reflect post-hoc reasoning, which means the model already knows the answer before reasoning. We therefore ask what CoT training is actually improving: is the model getting better at changing its action through generated reasoning, or is it getting better at predicting the action directly from the prompt? We study this question by comparing \emph{prompt actions} (predicting action without CoT) with CoT actions (predicting action with CoT). Across checkpoints, prompt-action quality improves substantially. While interacting with the environment, the relative advantage of CoT actions over prompt actions remains similar, showing that CoT training does not widen the advantage of CoT reasoning, and it helps to improve the quality of prompt actions. We further find that later checkpoints are less likely to revise the action in response to CoT, suggesting greater reliance on the prompt. Motivated by these patterns, we selectively mask action-token supervision on a fraction of training examples. This intervention improves out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [316] [Look-Before-Move: Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention in Dynamic 3D Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26964)
*Jiaming Bian,Bingliang Li,Yuehao Wu,Pichao Wang,Zhi Wang,Hailan Ma,Huadong Mo,Zhenhong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Look-Before-Move 框架，将动态3D场景中的摄像机规划分解为观察规约与运动执行两个阶段，通过语义观察合约、蒙特卡洛视点搜索和语义轨迹接地三个步骤，实现叙事驱动的主动视觉注意力规划，并在StoryBlender基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身AI与世界模型在动态3D环境中广泛应用，视觉感知需要从被动解释给定观察转向主动决定观察什么。现有方法通常直接生成摄像机运动，而忽略了"移动前先决定观察什么"这一关键能力——即摄像机作为具身观察者，需在叙事意图和物理3D约束下，自主决定观察对象、构图方式以及注意力随时间的转移。

Method: 提出 Look-Before-Move 框架，将摄像机规划分为三个阶段：(1) 语义观察合约（Semantic Observation Contract）：将导演意图转化为可执行的视觉约束（如主体、构图规则）；(2) 蒙特卡洛视点搜索（Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search）：在3D空间中搜索符合叙事要求且几何可行的视点；(3) 语义轨迹接地（Semantic Trajectory Grounding）：将离散视点连接为连续、无碰撞且时序连贯的摄像机运动轨迹。同时构建了基于StoryBlender的动态3D故事世界基准，包含50个故事、457个场景、1585个镜头。

Result: 在StoryBlender基准上的实验表明，该框架在主体感知质量、叙事意图一致性和轨迹质量三个维度上均优于代表性基线方法，验证了"在生成摄像机运动之前先组织视觉注意力"的重要性。

Conclusion: Look-Before-Move 通过将观察规约与运动执行解耦，使摄像机作为具身观察者能够根据叙事意图主动规划视觉注意力，为动态3D环境中的智能摄像机规划提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: As embodied AI and world models increasingly operate in dynamic 3D environments, visual perception must move beyond passively interpreting given observations toward actively deciding what to observe. We study this problem through camera planning in dynamic 3D story worlds, where the camera must not only generate smooth motion, but also decide what visual evidence should be acquired before it moves. We formulate this capability as Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention, where the camera acts as an embodied observer that determines what to observe, how to compose the observation, and how to shift attention over time under narrative intent and physical 3D constraints. To realize this capability, we propose Look-Before-Move, a camera planning framework that separates observation specification from motion execution. It first builds a Semantic Observation Contract to convert directorial intent into executable visual constraints, then performs Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search to find narrative-compliant and geometrically feasible viewpoints, and finally applies Semantic Trajectory Grounding to connect selected viewpoints into continuous, collision-aware, and temporally coherent camera motion. We further construct a dynamic 3D Story World Benchmark based on StoryBlender, covering 50 stories, 457 scenes, and 1585 shots with animated characters, semantic scene configurations, and executable 3D environments. Experiments show that our framework improves subject perception, intent consistency, and trajectory quality over representative baselines, demonstrating the importance of organizing visual attention before generating camera motion.

</details>


### [317] [Einstein World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26969)
*Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Zangir Iklassov,Ali Mekky,Zayd M. Kawakibi Zuhri,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出爱因斯坦世界模型（EWMs），一种将视觉-时序推演嵌入LLM推理轨迹的蓝图，使模型能通过"可视化思想实验"来辅助复杂推理，弥补纯文本推理的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者的核心关切是：某些复杂思维无法仅通过语言捕捉，特别是反事实事件的可视化能否作为语言之外的推理机制。驱动问题是：LLM能否被训练来利用这种可视化机制以提升推理能力。

Method: 提出Einstein World Models（EWMs）蓝图——在LLM推理轨迹中嵌入视觉-时序推演（visual-temporal rollouts）。具体地，LLM调用一个世界模块（world-module，区别于world model），生成所考虑场景的短视频推演。返回的推演不作为最终答案，而是作为可检验的假设，为后续推理提供支撑。这本质上是将LLM的工具调用能力（如网络搜索、代码执行）扩展到视觉思想实验领域。

Result: 论文处于蓝图/框架提出阶段，未报告具体实验数据，主要贡献在于概念架构的建立与论证。

Conclusion: EWMs将工具增强推理的范式从符号/代码域拓展至视觉域，为LLM提供了一种通过生成和检验视觉假设来进行复杂推理的新机制，暗示视觉模拟可作为语言之外的重要认知工具。

Abstract: Does intelligence require the ability to reason about phenomena beyond direct experience? It is natural to suspect that some complex thought cannot be captured through language alone. However, of particular concern to this work, is whether visualising counterfactual events can complement language as a mechanism for complex thought. We ask whether LLMs can be trained to utilise such visualisation mechanisms, in a way that benefits their reasoning abilities. Motivated by this question, we propose Einstein World Models. EWMs are a blueprint for LLM-based reasoning systems that place visual-temporal rollouts inside the reasoning trace, allowing them to reason in ways that text alone may not support well. In an EWM, the LLM calls a world-module (not to be confused with a world model), to produce short rollouts of scenes under consideration. The returned rollout is treated not as the answer, but as an inspectable hypothesis that can support later reasoning. Einstein World Models extend the capability of LLMs for tool calling (such as web search or code execution), into the domain of visual thought experiments.

</details>


### [318] [Adaptive Utility driven Resource Orchestration for Resilient AI (AURORA-AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27005)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Ilias Cherkaoui,Lizy Abraham,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AURORA-AI 提出了一种基于 HJB 反馈控制、Lyapunov 稳定性监测和公平性感知复合效用的自适应资源编排框架，能在非平稳条件下持续最大化包含预测性能、人口统计均等、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和可解释性的全局效用，实验表明其在黑天鹅事件恢复速度、分位数提升和公平性指标上均显著优于 Static、Round Robin、Greedy、LinUCB 及 PPO 等基线控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 系统在非平稳的计算、人口统计和运行条件下部署时，静态资源分配策略会导致预测性能和公平性、可解释性等以人为中心的属性同时退化，因此需要一种能在扰动下持续优化的自适应资源编排方法。

Method: 提出 AURORA-AI 框架，将 Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman 反馈控制、Lyapunov 稳定性监测与公平性感知复合效用函数统一为闭环策略。该复合效用联合建模预测性能、人口统计均等、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和可解释性，持续在异构 AI 模型群体间重分配计算预算以最大化全局效用。

Result: 在注入人口偏差冲击、渐进概念漂移和突发黑天鹅事件的离散时间仿真中，AURORA-AI 在黑天鹅事件后即时恢复（Static 需 88 步，PPO 需 22 步）；α-分位数和超分位数分别提升 29% 和 25%；同时降低平均和最大人口统计均等差距，并提高 Lyapunov 稳定运行步数占比。

Conclusion: 基于稳定性理论的公平性感知自适应编排是实现鲁棒、以人为中心 AI 部署的可行且有理论支撑的路径。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly deployed under non-stationary computational, demographic, and operational conditions in which static resource allocation strategies degrade both predictive performance and human-centric properties such as fairness and explainability. This paper presents AURORA-AI, an Adaptive Utility-driven Resource Orchestration framework for Resilient AI that unifies Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman feedback control, Lyapunov-based stability monitoring, and a fairness-aware composite utility into a single closed-loop policy.The framework continuously redistributes computational budget across a population of heterogeneous AI models so that the global utility, defined jointly over predictive performance, demographic parity, cost, latency, robustness, and interpretability, remains maximised under disruption. The framework is evaluated in a stress-rich discrete-time simulation that concurrently injects demographic bias shocks, gradual concept drift, and abrupt black-swan disruptions, and is compared against five established controllers including Static, Round Robin, Greedy, LinUCB, and a deep reinforcement-learning agent based on Proximal Policy Optimisation. AURORA-AI achieves immediate recovery from the black-swan event compared to eighty-eight time steps for the Static baseline and twenty-two for Proximal Policy Optimisation, lifts the alpha-quantile and the super-quantile by twenty-nine and twenty-five percent respectively, simultaneously reduces the mean and maximum demographic parity gap, and increases the fraction of Lyapunov-stable operating steps. These results indicate that fairness-aware adaptive orchestration grounded in stability theory is a practical and theoretically motivated path toward resilient human-centric AI deployment.

</details>


### [319] [Semantic Early-Stopping for Iterative LLM Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27009)
*Sahil Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体LLM循环中的语义早停问题，提出基于连续草稿嵌入的语义相似度和质量变化来判断停止时机的方法，替代传统的固定迭代次数截断。研究发现无评判器的语义停止器可在保持质量的前提下减少38%的运算token，而质量门控变体因逐轮评判成本过高反而适得其反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM循环（如Writer-Critic架构）几乎都采用固定迭代上限（max_iterations）作为终止条件，这是一个"语法级"的终止开关——它无法感知答案是否仍在改进，导致简单输入浪费token而困难输入被过早截断。作者旨在研究"语义早停"（semantic early-stopping），即根据草稿语义是否仍在变化以及答案质量是否仍在提升来决定何时停止。

Method: 提出了三层贡献：(1) 理论层面，证明了确定性终止性和良定义性，并将距离序列的收敛作为经验检验的猜想而非Banach压缩映射来处理；(2) 评估协议层面，设计了一种评判器高效的评估方法：对每个问题仅生成一次完整轨迹，在该轨迹上重放所有停止策略，并缓存所有LLM评判器调用，实现严格配对的效率-质量比较，同时将操作token与评估token分开核算；(3) 实证层面，在HotpotQA多跳检索增强问答数据集上进行实验，包含60个问题的测试集。

Result: 在HotpotQA测试集上，无评判器的语义停止器在质量持平（Δ-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81）的情况下，相比max_iterations减少了38%的操作token。而完整质量门控变体因每轮评判的token开销过大，反而适得其反。Oracle（选择最优轮次）的信息得分比所有实际策略高出+0.115（p ≈ 4e-11），表明问题的核心应从"何时停止"转向"哪一轮最佳"。

Conclusion: 语义早停可以在不牺牲质量的前提下显著降低多智能体LLM循环的计算成本，但质量门控机制因评判开销过大而不实用。核心挑战并非决定何时停止，而是识别哪一轮输出最优，这为后续研究指明了新方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) loops, for example a Writer that drafts and a Critic that revises, are almost always terminated by a fixed iteration cap (max_iterations). This is a syntactic kill-switch: it is blind to whether the answer is still improving, so it over-spends tokens on easy inputs and truncates hard ones. We study semantic early-stopping: the loop halts when consecutive draft embeddings stop changing in meaning (cosine distance with a patience window) and the answer's measured quality stops improving. Our work makes three contributions. First, an honest theoretical footing: we prove deterministic termination and well-definedness and machine-check these claims, while treating the convergence of the distance sequence as an empirically tested conjecture rather than a (previously over-claimed) Banach contraction. Second, a judge-efficient evaluation protocol: we generate each question's full trajectory once, replay every stopping policy over the identical drafts, and cache every LLM-judge call, yielding a strictly paired efficiency-versus-quality comparison at low cost; we further separate operational tokens (charged to a policy) from evaluation tokens (a measurement instrument). Third, an empirical study on multi-hop retrieval-augmented question answering (HotpotQA). On the 60-question test split, a judge-free semantic stopper reduces operational tokens by 38% relative to max_iterations at parity quality (Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81), whereas the full quality-gated variant is counter-productive because its per-round judging dominates cost. An oracle that selects the best round attains +0.115 Information Score over every practical policy (p ~ 4e-11), reframing the problem from "when to stop" (easy) to "which round is best" (open).

</details>


### [320] [How to evaluate clustering with ground truth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27061)
*Pasi Fränti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了用于聚类评估的外部有效性指标，重点分析了基于集合匹配的度量方法。作者推荐使用质心指数（CI）作为直观的簇级度量，而在需要点级细粒度度量时，推荐对簇大小无偏的配对集指数（PSI）或让所有点等权重的聚类准确率（ACC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有真实标签的情况下，如何选择合适的聚类外部评价指标是一个重要问题。现有指标众多，但缺乏对基于集合匹配度量的系统梳理和推荐指南，研究者常面临选择困难。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，回顾了最常见的外部效度指数，重点聚焦于基于集合匹配（set-matching-based）的度量指标，并对其进行分类比较：簇级（cluster-level）与点级（point-level）度量，以及对簇大小敏感与否的特性分析。

Result: 主要得出三条推荐结论：(1) 质心指数（CI）是直观的簇级度量，结果可解释性强；(2) 配对集指数（PSI）提供对簇大小无偏的归一化得分；(3) 若需所有点等权重，则聚类准确率（ACC）或其他集合匹配度量是合适选择。

Conclusion: 聚类外部评估指标的选择应取决于具体需求：粗粒度的簇级评估首选质心指数（CI）；细粒度的点级评估中，若需消除簇大小偏差则选配对集指数（PSI），若需各点等权则选聚类准确率（ACC）。

Abstract: External indexes can be used for cluster evaluation when ground truth is available. We review the most common external validity indexes focusing on set-matching-based measures. We recommend centroid index (CI), because it is an intuitive cluster-level measure with an explainable result. If we need a more fine-tuned, point-level measure, there are more choices. Pair-set index (PSI) provides a normalized score which is not biased by cluster sizes. If all points should matter equally, then clustering accuracy (ACC) or any other set-matching measure is suitable.

</details>


### [321] [Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27136)
*Shicheng Ye,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JERP 提出一种联合学习框架，让 LLM 智能体同时从交互轨迹中更新经验规则库和策略模型，使规则库与策略保持对齐，在 AlfWorld 和 WebShop 上取得一致的决策性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将交互经验的使用分为两条分离路径：外部自然语言规则提示（可解释但易与演化策略脱节）或轨迹微调模型参数（泛化好但难以修正稀疏奖励下的局部错误）。需要一种能兼顾两者优势的方法。

Method: JERP 维护一个长期经验规则池，决策时检索任务相关规则与交互历史一起作为条件输入给智能体；每轮 episode 后，利用收集的轨迹同时优化策略并通过对比当前 rollout 与参考成功轨迹来修订规则池，实现规则库与策略的协同演化。

Result: 在 AlfWorld 和 WebShop 两个复杂交互任务基准上，JERP 均取得了稳定的决策性能提升。

Conclusion: JERP 通过联合学习经验规则与策略，将稳定有效的行为逐步内化到模型本身，有效解决了规则-策略不同步的问题，为 LLM 智能体在多步交互环境中的经验利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: For LLM agents in multi-step interactive environments, a key challenge is to make effective use of accumulated interaction experience. Existing work has typically separated two uses of such experience: keeping it outside the model as natural-language rules for later prompting, or using trajectories and feedback to update the model parameters. The former is easy to interpret but can fall out of sync with the evolving policy; the latter improves the policy more broadly but provides only limited correction for local mistakes in sparse-reward settings. We present Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for LLM Agents (JERP), which updates a long-term experiential-rule pool and the policy from the same interaction trajectories. At decision time, JERP retrieves task-relevant rules and conditions the agent on them together with the interaction history. After each episode, it uses the collected trajectories both to optimize the policy and to revise the rule pool by comparing current rollouts with reference successful trajectories. This coupling keeps the rule pool aligned with the evolving policy while allowing stable and effective behaviors to be gradually absorbed into the model itself. Experiments on AlfWorld and WebShop show that JERP yields consistent gains in decision performance for complex interactive tasks.

</details>


### [322] [OpenRCA 2.0: From Outcome Labels to Causal Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27154)
*Aoyang Fang,Yifan Yang,Jin'ao Shang,Qisheng Lu,Junjielung Xu,Rui Wang,Songhan Zhang,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Yu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有RCA基准数据集仅标注根因，缺失从根因到症状的因果传播路径，导致评估退化为简单的模式匹配。本文提出PAVE逐步标注协议，利用故障注入建立正向因果链路，构建了OpenRCA 2.0（500个实例），为首个具备逐步因果标注的跨系统RCA基准。11个前沿LLM的评估表明，精确根因集恢复率仅20.7%；虽76.0%的案例中Agent能识别至少一个正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将其锚定在可验证的因果传播路径上——这一"未锚定诊断"问题在仅看结果的评估中被掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based RCA评估基准存在根本性缺陷：仅标注根因而非因果传播路径，导致任务被简化为从症状到根因的模式匹配，无法真实测试LLM Agent的长期上下文理解、多步推理与工具使用能力。

Method: 提出PAVE（逐步标注协议）：采用"前向验证"机制，利用故障注入中已知的干预操作，从原因向结果方向重建因果传播路径，而非从症状反向推断。基于PAVE构建OpenRCA 2.0，包含500个跨系统实例及逐步因果标注。

Result: （1）11个前沿LLM在精确恢复根因集上平均成功率仅20.7%；（2）放宽标准后，76.0%案例中Agent能识别至少一个正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将其锚定在可验证的因果传播路径上，暴露了"未锚定诊断"的失败模式。

Conclusion: 仅关注最终结果的评估方式掩盖了LLM Agent在根因分析中的关键失败模式——未锚定诊断。逐步因果标注是构建可信LLM-based RCA Agent所缺失的关键要素。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) poses a holistic test of LLM agentic capabilities, such as long-context understanding, multi-step reasoning, and tool use. However, existing datasets suffer from a fundamental gap: they label only the root cause, not the propagation path connecting it to the observed symptom, which largely simplifies the task to naive pattern matching. To support rigorous evaluation, we introduce PAVE, a step-wise labeling protocol that leverages known interventions from fault injection to reconstruct causal propagation paths. The mechanism is forward verification: reasoning from cause to effect rather than inferring backward from symptoms. Applying PAVE yields OpenRCA 2.0 (500 instances), the first cross-system RCA benchmark with step-wise causal annotations for LLM agents. Across 11 frontier LLMs, recovering the exact root-cause set succeeds in only 20.7% of cases on average. To locate where this difficulty lies, we relax the criterion and find what we call the ungrounded diagnosis: agents identify at least one correct root-cause service in 76.0% of cases, but ground that service in a verified causal propagation path to the observed symptom in only 61.5%. Outcome-only evaluation hides this failure mode; step-wise causal ground truth is the missing piece for trustworthy LLM-based RCA agents.

</details>


### [323] [TOPS: First-Principles Visual Token Pruning via Constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets for Efficient MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27161)
*Tinghao Wang,Yichen Guo,Rui Huang,Zheng Lu,Qizhe Zhang,Chenxi Li,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Zhirong Shen,Yaosong Du,Guangyan Gan,Wenya Wang,Lin William Cong,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，将视觉token剪枝形式化为构建"Token最优保留集"问题，通过信息论分析提出三大原则：任务相关性、信息覆盖率和语义多样性。基于此提出TOPS——一种无需训练、模型无关的剪枝模块，在7种MLLM和14个基准上超越现有方法，可在移除77.8%视觉token的情况下保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLM）因大量视觉token导致计算开销过大，而现有剪枝方法存在缺陷：基于注意力的标准倾向于保留冗余token，基于多样性的标准则对用户指令不敏感，即便组合多种标准的方法也缺乏对token剪枝内在目标的原则性形式化表述。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，将视觉token剪枝形式化为构建Token最优保留集（Token Optimal Preservation Sets, TOPS）。通过自顶向下的信息论分析，确立三项基本原则：1）任务相关性（Task Relevance）——保留与用户指令最相关的token；2）信息覆盖率（Information Coverage）——保留的token应充分覆盖图像中的信息；3）语义多样性（Semantic Diversity）——避免保留语义高度冗余的token。基于这三原则设计TOPS剪枝模块，该模块无需训练、与模型架构无关，可即插即用于各类MLLM。

Result: 在7种MLLM骨干网络和14个基准数据集上进行广泛实验，TOPS在多种剪枝设置下均超越先前方法。在LLaVA-NeXT上，TOPS移除77.8%的视觉token后，在7B和13B模型上分别保持了100.0%和100.6%的性能，表明剪枝冗余视觉token不仅可以维持性能，有时还能减轻幻觉现象。

Conclusion: TOPS通过原则性的信息论框架统一了视觉token剪枝的核心目标，实验证明大幅剪枝冗余token不仅可行，甚至可能提升模型表现。这为未来轻量化MLLM设计提供了新的思路——冗余token的移除有助于缓解幻觉问题。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens, which introduces substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning offers a natural solution, yet existing methods are imperfect: attention-based criteria tend to retain redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria are often agnostic to user instructions. Even methods that combine multiple criteria still lack a principled formulation of the intrinsic objective of token pruning. In this paper, we revisit visual token pruning from a first-principles perspective and formulate it as constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Through a top-down information-theoretic analysis, we identify three fundamental principles for effective token selection: Task Relevance, Information Coverage, and Semantic Diversity. Based on these principles, we propose TOPS, a training-free and model-agnostic pruning module that can be applied to various MLLMs. Extensive experiments on 7 MLLM backbones and 14 benchmarks demonstrate that TOPS outperforms prior methods under diverse pruning settings. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, TOPS removes 77.8% of visual tokens while preserving 100.0% and 100.6% performance on its 7B and 13B models, respectively, suggesting that pruning redundant visual tokens can sometimes mitigate hallucination and inspire future lightweight MLLM design.

</details>


### [324] [A Process Harness for Uplifting Legacy Workflows to Agentic BPM: Design and Realization in CUGA FLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27188)
*Fabiana Fournier,Lior Limonad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“流程马具”（Process Harness）机制，在不替换底层确定性工作流引擎的前提下，通过策略治理的智能体层（TaskAgent、DecisionAgent、FlowAgent）在关键控制点注入推理、适配与监督能力，实现遗留工作流向智能体化BPM的升级，并以TDF模型和CUGA FLO系统在贷款审批场景中验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPM依赖确定性工作流引擎，虽能保证结构化合规（命令式需求），但缺乏应对知识密集型任务、动态决策和运行时流程适配的灵活性（规范性需求）。现有方案往往要求替换整个引擎，成本高、风险大。因此，需要一种在不更换底层引擎的前提下，将智能体能力注入遗留工作流的机制。

Method: 1）提出Process Harness架构：在确定性工作流引擎外围包裹策略治理的智能体层，通过拦截指定控制点实现推理注入。2）建立TDF（Task-Decision-Flow）模型：定义数据模式与执行语义，将LLM推理分解为三类策略治理的智能体——TaskAgent（知识密集型任务执行）、DecisionAgent（逐案例网关路由）、FlowAgent（基于Hook机制的运行时流程适配）。所有智能体均受统一策略集FRAME约束。3）实现CUGA FLO系统，并在贷款审批工作流上进行演示验证。

Result: CUGA FLO系统成功在贷款审批场景中展示了三类智能体及Hook驱动监管覆写的协同运作。Process Harness实现了命令式需求（确定性工作流保障结构合规）与规范性需求（策略框架下的智能体自主性）的独特调和。

Conclusion: Process Harness提供了一种新颖的Agentic BPM升级路径，在不替换遗留引擎的前提下，通过策略治理的智能体层在控制点注入推理与适应能力，兼顾了结构合规性与智能灵活性。TDF模型为该机制提供了严格的形式化基础。

Abstract: We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.

</details>


### [325] [Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27215)
*Manjinder Singh,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Mohamed Elawady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAversary，一种基于混合遗传算法的黑盒对抗攻击方法，仅需模型输出的logit值即可生成对抗样本。通过GloVe嵌入引导变异操作来提升语义相似度。实验表明，该方法在多个基准上显著降低目标模型准确率（如从76.8%降至5.8%），优于BAE和A2T，但代价是扰动词数翻倍、语义相似度略低、运行时间增加约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP对抗攻击方法日益精确，但往往需要模型内部结构信息（白盒），且攻击效果与语义保持性之间存在权衡。作者旨在提出一种完全不依赖模型内部结构的黑盒方法，仅用输出logit引导搜索，同时保证对抗样本的语义相似性和攻击有效性。

Method: 提出GAversary：一种混合遗传算法框架。将目标模型视为黑盒，仅利用logit输出值作为适应度信号。核心创新在于采用GloVe词嵌入来生成候选替换词作为变异算子，以维持语义相似度。算法通过遗传迭代（选择、交叉、变异）不断优化对抗样本，逐步替换原文中的关键token，直至模型分类错误。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和知名目标模型上进行评估：（1）最佳情况下将模型准确率从76.8%降至5.8%，远优于BAE的27.6%；（2）大幅超越BAE和A2T两种对比攻击方法。代价方面：扰动词数约为对比方法的两倍，语义相似度略低于BAE/A2T，运行时间增加约5%。

Conclusion: GAversary证明了仅依靠模型logit输出即可实现高效的黑盒对抗攻击，GloVe嵌入引导的变异策略在攻击强度上取得了显著优势。该方法在攻击效果与语义保持、计算开销之间存在可接受的权衡，为NLP黑盒对抗攻击提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.

</details>


### [326] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BINEVAL将LLM评估分解为原子化二元问题，生成可解释的多维分数，在多个基准上匹配或超越强基线，并支持迭代式提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM输出评估存在三大瓶颈：人工评估昂贵且慢、词汇指标与开放生成的人类判断相关性差、整体性LLM评判器产生的分数不透明且难以调试。需要一种透明、可诊断且可操作的评估框架。

Method: BINEVAL框架通过两步实现：首先，给定任务提示后，元提示（meta-prompt）自动生成细粒度的原子二元评估问题；然后，LLM对每个输出独立回答这些问题，汇总二元裁决得到校准后的多维总分。评估过程同时在问题层面输出透明反馈。

Result: 在SummEval、Topical-Chat和QAGS基准上，BINEVAL匹配或超越UniEval和G-Eval等强基线，在QAGS事实一致性基准上表现尤为突出。BINEVAL更好地匹配人类评分分布，避免了常见的天花板效应，能更好区分边界输出与明显缺陷输出。问题层面的反馈还支持迭代提示优化，在摘要评估提示和IFBench生成提示上均有效，支持自更新和跨模型更新两种设置。

Conclusion: BINEVAL是一个任务无关、无需训练、可解释的评估框架，兼具强实证性能与实用的诊断和优化价值。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [327] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EO-WM 是一个面向多光谱地球观测预测的视频扩散Transformer模型，通过物理信息条件框架（气候基线、天气异常、累积物理胁迫）建模天气驱动的未来地表动态，并引入极端夏季和季节配对两个诊断基准来评估天气响应行为，在NDVI衰减幅度和方向命中率上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EO预测方法存在两个核心缺陷：确定性模型将不确定性坍缩为单一预测，扩散模型将天气变量视为无差别的条件信号，且现有基准侧重重建精度而非预测对变化天气强迫的响应正确性。作者旨在解决这一部分观测、天气驱动的世界建模问题。

Method: 提出EO-WM视频扩散Transformer，核心创新在于物理信息条件框架：(1) 将气象强迫分解为气候基线、天气异常和累积物理胁迫信号三个分量；(2) 通过分离的条件通路分别处理基线和异常；(3) 对异常强迫进行时间累积以捕获持续的热胁迫和干旱胁迫。此外，引入两个诊断基准：极端夏季基准（评估极端天气下植被退化的严重度感知预测）和季节配对基准（测试变化天气强迫下的响应保真度）。

Result: 在NDVI衰减幅度预测上相对误差降低5.63%，方向命中率相对提升7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: EO-WM通过物理信息条件框架有效建模了天气驱动的EO预测问题，显著提升了模型对天气强迫变化的响应能力，两个新基准为评估预测模型的天气响应行为提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


### [328] [Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27286)
*Alina Bazarova,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Henrik Zunker,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Torben Heinsohn,Wolfgang Wiechert,Katharina Noeh,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于模拟的推断（SBI）作为MCMC贝叶斯校准的可扩展替代方案，应用于COVID-19德国ICU数据的SECIR动力学模型。结果表明SBI在保持后验一致性的同时，将计算时间从MCMC的约1000秒（31天）和19000秒（201天）分别降至60-70秒和157秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MCMC方法用于流行病动力学模型的贝叶斯校准时，在高维非线性系统中计算成本极高，尤其难以满足近实时重复分析的需求。因此需要一种计算效率更高的替代方法。

Method: 采用神经后验估计（NPE）的SBI框架，对SECIR动力学模型进行贝叶斯校准。使用2020年德国COVID-19 ICU占用数据进行实验，设置了31天滑动推理窗口和多达201天的长周期重建任务（含多个传播率变化点）。通过Wasserstein距离、KL散度及后验预测检验与MCMC进行定量对比。

Result: 31天窗口：SBI与MCMC的后验分布高度一致，准确重现ICU轨迹；201天任务：SBI尽管不确定性增加，仍保持了主导的后验结构。计算效率：31天任务中MCMC约需1000秒，SBI单GPU仅需60-70秒；201天任务中MCMC超19000秒，SBI仅需157秒。

Conclusion: SBI为流行病动力学模型的贝叶斯校准提供了快速且计算高效的框架，能够支持近实时重复推断和快速疫情分析，具有替代传统MCMC的实用价值。

Abstract: Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.

</details>


### [329] [Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27287)
*Preet Baxi,Jiannan Xu,Jane Yi Jiang,Stefanus Jasin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了LLM简历筛选中的提示注入攻击：当注入行为稀少且候选人质量相近时效果显著，但随着注入普及或质量差异拉大则迅速失效，且可能引发公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于自动化简历筛选与排名，这催生了候选人策略性操纵系统的动机。研究者旨在系统性地揭示提示注入（prompt injection）在这种场景下的有效性边界及其对公平性的影响。

Method: 通过受控实验，在候选人质量同质化和异质化两种条件下，模拟不同比例的候选人使用提示注入（即在简历中加入不涉及新资质的自我推销性文本），观察其对LLM排名结果的影响。

Result: （1）同质化场景且少数人注入时，提示注入能可靠提升排名；（2）当多数人都注入时，注入效果崩溃；（3）异质化场景中，注入平均效果减弱，但偶尔可使低质量候选人超越高质量候选人，引发公平性担忧。

Conclusion: LLM简历筛选在操纵行为稀少且候选人质量差异小时最为脆弱。研究揭示了提示注入作为一种低成本策略操纵手段的风险边界，为设计更鲁棒的算法招聘系统提供了警示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26

</details>


### [330] [When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模型LLM系统的精度增益受限于"所有模型同时出错率"beta，而非通常报告的成对错误相关性rho。通过67个模型的实证分析，发现实际beta显著高于高斯copula模型的预测值，且组合模型的增益主要来自模型在不同问题上出错，而非简单堆叠更多模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模型LLM系统（路由、投票、级联、融合等）的研究普遍关注成对错误相关性rho，但忽略了"所有模型在同一查询上同时出错"的beta指标。本文旨在揭示这一被忽视的指标才是限制多模型系统精度增益的根本上限，并质疑现有benchmark在评估组合系统时的充分性。

Method: 理论推导：证明对于输出为单一模型答案的任意策略，准确率上界为1-beta。统计推断：使用Clopper-Pearson置信区间对beta进行有限样本约束。实证分析：在67个模型（21家提供商）上，使用tetrachoric校准的单因子模型和高斯copula模型估计beta，并在开放数学题、代码执行题、GPQA-Diamond自由回答和选择题上进行验证。引入五法官panel（kappa 0.73-0.92）评估自由回答质量。

Result: 67模型高斯copula模型系统性低估了全错尾部风险（数学题：观察beta=0.052 vs 预测0.023，低估约2.5倍，90%CI 1.7-3.4）。代码题beta=0.079。GPQA-Diamond自由回答beta=0.127，表明共失效根源于答案格式而非学科。低rho异构集成优于高rho的Self-MoA，但在可检验任务上，若无强查询级路由信号，组合模型很少超越单一最佳模型。

Conclusion: 多模型系统的真实增益上限由beta决定而非rho。实践中模型共失效概率被系统性低估，组合收益主要源于模型在不同问题上出错（即错误模式互补），而非模型数量增加。在缺乏强路由信号时，简单组合多个模型未必优于使用单一最佳模型。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router.
  Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.

</details>


### [331] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言的数字孪生框架，利用大语言模型模拟老年人的对话行为，结合风格计量线索和上下文元数据，并通过多头条件变分自编码器评估重建质量与认知评分预测，在I-CONECT数据集上验证了其用于轻度认知障碍个性化监测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻度认知障碍（MCI）的早期检测仍具挑战性，语言和对话模式可作为非侵入性生物标志物，但现有方法缺乏个性化且持续的认知健康监测手段。数字孪生为此提供了新范式，但如何构建能忠实反映个体认知状态的语言数字孪生尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出基于语言的数字孪生框架：(1) 利用大语言模型（LLM）结合风格计量（stylometric）线索和上下文元数据，模拟老年人的对话行为；(2) 引入多头条件变分自编码器（multi-head cVAE），联合衡量对话重建质量并预测蒙特利尔认知评估（MoCA）分数，以评估数字孪生的保真度和认知一致性。

Result: 在I-CONECT数据集上的实验表明：该数字孪生能够保留个体特异性特征，其重建误差和MoCA预测误差与真实数据相当，且显著优于基线GPT生成的响应。

Conclusion: 基于语言的数字孪生有望成为一种可扩展、非侵入式的个性化连续认知健康监测方法，在MCI早期检测和认知健康追踪方面具有较大潜力。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [332] [Explainable Outlier Detection for Interval-valued Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26307)
*Catarina P. Loureiro,M. Rosário Oliveira,Paula Brito,Lina Oliveira*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Shapley值的可解释性方法，用于解释区间值数据的异常检测结果。作者推导了稳健区间马氏距离平方的Shapley值闭合解，可将异常分解为中心、范围和交叉项的贡献，并扩展至Shapley交互指数以捕捉变量间的成对交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测的可解释性日益重要，但对于区间值数据这类复杂数据结构，现有研究几乎空白。传统异常检测方法仅能识别异常观测，无法解释哪些变量（或变量的哪些方面）驱动了异常行为。

Method: 基于区间最小协方差行列式（Interval MCD）估计器的异常检测框架，推导稳健区间马氏距离平方的Shapley值闭合表达式，实现变量贡献的高效计算；将异常分解为中心贡献、范围贡献和交叉项贡献；进一步引入Shapley交互指数捕捉成对变量交互对异常行为的驱动效应。

Result: Shapley值的闭合解使得区间值异常的解释可精确分解为各变量的中心、范围及交叉项贡献；该方法可识别在多元层面不明显但在单变量层面显著的cellwise异常；通过两个真实数据集验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法为区间值数据的异常检测提供了细粒度的可解释性，Shapley值分解不仅能定位驱动异常的变量，还能区分是中心、范围还是交叉项导致的异常，结合交互指数进一步揭示变量间的联合异常模式。

Abstract: Explainability is increasingly recognized as a key aspect of outlier detection. However, for complex data structures such as interval-valued data, it remains largely unexplored. Building on an outlier detection framework based on the Interval Minimum Covariance Determinant estimator, we propose a novel approach to explain the outlyingness of interval-valued observations using the concept of the Shapley value. We derive a closed-form expression for the Shapley value of the squared robust Interval-Mahalanobis distance, enabling efficient computation of variable contributions. This formulation allows for a fine-grained interpretation of outliers, providing a detailed decomposition into contributions from centers, ranges, and cross-terms of the interval-valued observations. Moreover, the Shapley value is closely connected to the concept of cellwise outliers, as it can help identify variable-specific outliers that may not be evident at multivariate level. We further extend the framework through the Shapley interaction index to capture pairwise variable interactions driving atypical behavior. The practical utility of the proposed approach is illustrated through two real-world datasets.

</details>


### [333] [Variance Deltas for Visualizing and Explaining Posterior Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26309)
*Collin Cademartori*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交互式可视化系统"方差增量"（variance deltas），用于从贝叶斯后验分布中发现缺失信息。该系统将未观测的模型量组织为树状结构，按其对目标量不确定性的解释能力排序，支持自动生成候选子集及交互式操作，帮助研究者高效识别哪些未观测信息一旦获得即可显著降低不确定性。论文在因果推断模拟数据和真实民调预测数据上展示了该系统的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在观测性研究中，数据生成过程和样本量不可控，无法先验保证估计精度。即使数据不足以确定目标量，它们仍可能揭示"缺失信息"——即那些若能观测到就能显著降低不确定性的未观测变量。现有方法缺乏系统性地从后验分布中发现和解释此类缺失信息的工具，这驱动了本文的研究。

Method: 提出"方差增量"（variance deltas）交互式可视化系统：1）将缺失信息表示为未观测模型量的子集；2）按各子集对目标量不确定性的解释程度（即条件方差缩减量），将其组织为树状结构；3）从最小用户输入自动构建候选子集；4）实现子集分割与合并的交互操作，支持高效探索。系统以软件包形式提供。

Result: 在两项应用中验证了系统的有效性：(1) 模拟因果推断问题中，发现治疗效应参数的非显而易见的缺失信息来源；(2) 基于真实民调数据的预测模型中，识别出众多偏差来源下总体比例估计的缺失信息，发现了有意义的解释。

Conclusion: 方差增量系统能够帮助研究者在观测性研究中系统性地发现和解释后验不确定性背后的"缺失信息"，即便在数据不足以精确估计目标量时，也能明确需要补充哪些信息来降低不确定性，为后续数据收集和模型改进提供指导。

Abstract: In observational settings, where the data generating process and possibly the sample size are not controlled, it is typically impossible to guarantee a priori that quantities of interest will be estimated with sufficient precision. However, even when the data do not determine the quantities of interest, they may still allow determination of what is missing -- unobserved information which, if observed, would meaningfully reduce uncertainty. We propose an interactive visualization system, termed variance deltas, to enable the discovery of such missing information from a Bayesian posterior distribution. This system, which we provide as a software package, represents missing information as subsets of unobserved model quantities, organized into a tree based on how well each subset explains uncertainty about the quantity of interest. This system both automates the construction of candidate subsets from minimal user input and implements interactive operations for the division and combination of subsets, allowing the efficient discovery of interesting and useful explanations. We demonstrate this system by using it to discover nonobvious explanations of uncertainty for (1) a treatment effect parameter in a simulated causal inference problem and (2) a population proportion in a forecasting model of real polling data with many sources of bias.

</details>


### [334] [A unified approach to outlier identification for mixed-type data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26324)
*Efthymios Costa,Christian Hennig*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种面向混合类型数据（连续变量+有序变量）的离群点识别方法，基于稳健最小协方差行列式（MCD）估计器和潜在高斯模型，并通过理论分析和仿真实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集中常同时包含连续变量和有序变量（如评分、等级），但现有离群点检测方法多仅适用于单一类型数据。需要一种能统一处理混合类型数据、且对离群点具有稳健性的识别方法。

Method: 1）假设非离群数据服从多元高斯分布，有序变量则假设存在潜在高斯变量；2）采用稳健的MCD估计器估计非离群数据的分布参数；3）将MCD扩展以处理有序变量下潜在高斯信息不可直接观测的问题；4）给出了崩溃点理论分析，证明替换观测值不会阻止足够极端的离群点被识别。

Result: 在多种污染类型的合成数据仿真中，该方法实现了高检测率和低误报率。在Airbnb房源数据（含连续和有序属性）的实际应用中，验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效处理混合连续-有序类型数据的离群点检测问题，兼具理论保证（崩溃点定理）和实际应用效果，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: We present an outlier identification method for mixed type data sets comprising continuous and ordinal variables. We define outliers based on using a multivariate Gaussian distribution as reference distribution for non-outliers, with a latent Gaussian assumed for ordinal variables. The proposed algorithm is based on the robust Minimum Covariance Determinant estimator for estimating the parameters of the multivariate Gaussian for the non-outliers. This is extended to account for the fact that the full Gaussian information underlying the ordinal variables is not observed. A breakdown theorem shows that replacing observations will noty stop extreme enough outliers from being identified. The effectiveness of our approach is demonstrated via simulations on synthetic data with various types of contamination, achieving high detection and low false positive rates. Practical relevance is illustrated through an application to Airbnb listing data containing both continuous and ordinal attributes.

</details>


### [335] [Incomplete Matrix Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26325)
*Khaled Fouda,Aurélie Labbe,Karim Oualkacha*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出Incomplete Matrix Regression (IMR)，一个融合行/列协变量信息的分布无关惩罚回归框架，用于矩阵补全。该模型将目标矩阵分解为截距项、Lasso正则化的协变量效应、以及低秩潜成分，并通过岭型惩罚融入已知相似性结构。作者提供了可扩展的交替最小二乘算法，建立了非渐近误差界，并通过模拟和实际数据验证了预测精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多应用（如推荐系统、城市出行）中，待补全的矩阵往往伴随行/列辅助协变量，且存在行间或列间的依赖结构。现有矩阵补全方法未能有效整合这些辅助信息与已知相似性结构，因此需要一个统一的框架来同时利用协变量信息和结构依赖，提升补全精度和计算效率。

Method: 提出IMR框架：目标矩阵 = 截距 + 协变量效应（Lasso惩罚） + 低秩潜成分（核范数或因子分解形式），其中潜成分可通过空间/时间核等已知相似性结构施加岭型惩罚。估计采用模块化交替最小二乘算法，支持灵活增删模型组件而无需重新推导更新公式。理论上为非渐近误差界提供证明，分别对齐Lasso和矩阵补全的标准收敛率。

Result: 模拟研究和两个真实数据应用表明，IMR在预测精度上可与更复杂的方法媲美，且计算成本仅为后者的一小部分。方法已实现为R包IMR。

Conclusion: IMR为矩阵补全提供了一个灵活、高效且理论有保障的统一框架，能够有机整合协变量和已知相似性结构，在保证预测精度的同时显著降低计算开销，具有较好的实用价值和可扩展性。

Abstract: Matrix completion seeks to recover a low-rank matrix from a sparse and noisy subset of its entries. In many applications, such as recommendation systems and urban mobility, the observed matrix is accompanied by auxiliary covariates on its rows and columns and exhibits dependence across them. We propose Incomplete Matrix Regression (IMR), a distribution-free penalized regression framework that integrates such information into matrix completion. The target matrix is modeled as the sum of intercepts, covariate effects regularized by a Lasso penalty, and a low-rank latent component that captures structure unexplained by the covariates. Known similarity structures, such as spatial and temporal kernels, are incorporated through ridge-type penalties on the latent factors. For estimation, we provide a scalable alternating least-squares algorithm whose modular form allows us to include or exclude individual model components without rederiving the updates. We establish non-asymptotic error bounds for both the Lasso and matrix completion estimators that are consistent with standard rates in their respective literature. Through simulation studies and two real-data applications, we demonstrate that the proposed method attains predictive accuracy competitive with more complex methods at a small fraction of their computational cost. The methodology is implemented in the R package IMR.

</details>


### [336] [Robust Estimation of Polychoric Correlation for Complex Survey Designs Using Minimum Divergence Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26351)
*Siqi Wei,David Kepplinger,Anand N. Vidyashankar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对复杂抽样调查中多分格相关系数（polychoric correlation）的稳健估计问题，提出了基于Hellinger距离（HD）和负指数差异（NED）两种最小散度准则的稳健估计量。HD方法引入Ridge和Lasso惩罚项以正则化冗余参数，同时保持相关系数不受惩罚，并建立了考虑抽样设计的一致性及渐近正态性理论；NED方法则在特定污染模式下表现更优。模拟研究系统比较了两类方法在多种污染几何形态下的表现，为实际调查数据分析提供了方法选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准极大似然估计对调查数据中的污染（如回答误差、访员效应、粗心作答）高度敏感，但对所有观测赋予等权重。在复杂抽样设计下，现有方法缺乏既能处理污染又能纳入抽样权重的稳健估计框架，亟需发展新的稳健多分格相关系数估计方法。

Method: 作者基于Hellinger距离（HD）和负指数差异（NED）两种最小散度准则，通过Horvitz-Thompson调整的单元格频率纳入抽样权重，构建复杂调查设计下的稳健多分格相关系数估计量。HD估计量进一步引入岭回归和Lasso惩罚变体，仅对冗余参数正则化而保持相关系数不惩罚，并推导出一致性、渐近正态性及反映抽样设计的夹心协方差矩阵。模拟采用Poisson比例规模抽样，系统考察三种污染几何形态（一致上尾、一致下尾、不一致混合角点）与标准/非标准潜在边际分布的交叉组合。

Result: 惩罚HD方法在一致污染（concordant contamination）下具有最低均方误差；NED方法在不一致污染（discordant contamination）及复合模型误设-污染效应下表现最佳。HD的影响函数有限但非一致有界，反映其对稀疏单元格的敏感性。两类估计量形成互补优势。

Conclusion: 研究为复杂调查中多分格相关估计提供了两类互补的稳健方法：惩罚HD适用于预期为一致污染的场景，NED适用于预期为不一致污染的场景。作者给出了基于预期污染模式的方法选择实践指南。

Abstract: Standard maximum likelihood estimation of polychoric correlations is highly sensitive to contamination in survey data, including response errors, interviewer effects, and careless responding, yet assigns equal weight to all observations regardless of data quality. We develop robust estimators for polychoric correlation under complex survey designs based on two minimum divergence criteria -- Hellinger distance (HD) and negative exponential disparity (NED) -- incorporating survey weights through Horvitz--Thompson adjusted cell frequencies. For HD, we propose penalized Ridge and Lasso variants that regularize nuisance parameters while leaving the correlation unpenalized, and establish consistency and asymptotic normality with a sandwich covariance reflecting the sampling design. The influence function is finite but not uniformly bounded, reflecting Hellinger's sensitivity to sparse cells. Simulations under Poisson proportional-to-size sampling examine three contamination geometries -- concordant upper, concordant lower, and discordant mixed corner -- crossed with standard and non-standard latent marginals. The two estimator classes offer complementary advantages: penalized HD methods achieve the lowest mean squared error under concordant contamination, while NED performs best under discordant contamination and under compound misspecification--contamination effects. We provide practical guidelines for method selection based on anticipated contamination patterns in survey practice.

</details>


### [337] [Learning rate selection via weighted Fisher divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26478)
*Takahiro Onizuka*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对广义贝叶斯推断中的学习率选择问题，提出基于加权Fisher散度的校准方法，给出学习率的闭式表达式，该学习率在特定情形下不大于Fisher信息匹配学习率，并通过数值实验和实际数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义贝叶斯框架通过引入基于损失的似然提供了灵活的建模方式，但其灵活性带来了校准问题——尤其在模型误设下，传统的贝叶斯信息等式失效，未经校准的Gibbs后验所得的置信集缺乏正确的频率学派不确定性解释。因此，需要一种系统的方法来选择学习率以校准广义后验。

Method: 作者引入加权Fisher散度（weighted Fisher divergence），用于度量广义后验的渐近分布与具有夹心型方差（sandwich-type variance）的正态分布之间的差异。通过最小化该散度，推导出学习率的闭式表达式。该框架将Fisher信息匹配学习率作为特例包含其中。

Result: 所得学习率的闭式解在重要的特殊情形下不大于现有的Fisher信息匹配学习率。数值例子和真实数据分析均展示了所提方法在校准广义后验方面的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过加权Fisher散度方法选择学习率，可以有效解决广义贝叶斯推断在模型误设下的校准问题，为Gibbs后验提供更可靠的频率学派覆盖性质。

Abstract: The general Bayesian approach provides a flexible modeling framework by introducing a loss-based likelihood. A general posterior has a learning rate, which controls the relative weight of the loss-based likelihood and the prior. The flexibility of general Bayesian inference comes with an important calibration problem, especially under model misspecification. In such cases, the conventional Bayesian information identity fails, and credible sets derived from an uncalibrated Gibbs posterior need not have the desired uncertainty interpretation. This paper aims to select the learning rate used to calibrate a general posterior. By introducing the weighted Fisher divergence between the asymptotic distribution of the general posterior and a normal distribution with sandwich-type variance, we provide a closed-form expression for the selected learning rate. The selected learning rate includes the Fisher information matching learning rate as a special case and is no larger than it in an important special case. Numerical examples and a real data analysis demonstrate the usefulness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [338] [A Unified Three-Stage Weighting Framework for Causal Inference and Mediation Analysis under Case-Control Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26543)
*Tarikul Islam,Mahbub A. H. M. Latif*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的三阶段加权框架(3S-weighting)，用于病例-对照研究中的因果推断与因果中介分析：首先利用密度比学习和标签偏移校正估计人群结局患病率，其次构建基于患病率的设计权重重建目标人群分布，最后在边际结构模型框架内应用因果与中介权重，估计总效应、纯直接效应、纯间接效应及交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病例-对照研究因回顾性结局依赖抽样扭曲了人群结局分布，导致传统因果推断和中介分析方法产生严重偏倚。现有方法缺乏在病例-对照设计下统一处理总效应和路径特异性因果效应的框架。

Method: 提出三阶段加权框架：(1) 使用密度比学习(density-ratio learning)结合外部协变量信息与标签偏移校正(label-shift correction)估计未知的人群结局患病率；(2) 利用患病率为基础的设计权重将回顾性样本重建为目标人群分布；(3) 在边际结构模型(marginal structural model)框架中应用稳定化因果权重与中介权重，估计总效应及路径特异性效应（纯直接效应、纯间接效应、交互效应）。

Result: 模拟研究表明，忽略回顾性抽样的传统分析在总效应和中介效应估计中均产生显著偏倚；所提方法在不同抽样情境下能一致恢复目标人群因果参数。NHANES数据应用进一步验证了该框架的实际可操作性与效用。

Conclusion: 所提3S-weighting框架为病例-对照研究中的因果推断和中介分析提供了统一且有效的解决方案，能够纠正回顾性抽样带来的偏倚，具有广泛的流行病学和生物医学应用前景。

Abstract: Case-control studies are widely used in epidemiology and biomedical research because they provide substantial efficiency gains when outcomes are rare or prospective follow-up is impractical. However, retrospective outcome-dependent sampling distorts the population outcome distribution, creating fundamental challenges for causal inference. We propose a unified three-stage weighting (3S-weighting) framework for causal inference and causal mediation analysis from case--control studies. The proposed approach first estimates the unknown population outcome prevalence using density-ratio learning and label-shift correction combined with externally available covariate information. Next, prevalence-based design weights are used to reconstruct the target population distribution from the retrospective sample. Finally, stabilized causal and mediation weights are applied within a marginal structural modeling framework to estimate total and pathway-specific causal effects, including the pure direct effect, pure indirect effect, and interaction effect. Simulation studies demonstrate that conventional analyses that ignore retrospective sampling can produce substantial bias in both total and mediation effect estimates, whereas the proposed approach consistently recovers the target population causal parameters across a range of sampling scenarios. An application of data from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey further illustrates the practical implementation and utility of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [339] [Fast Robust Regression via Orthogonal Block Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26611)
*Anthony Christidis,Matias Salibian-Barrera*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对鲁棒回归中MM估计量的初始点选取问题，本文提出了一种基于分块坐标下降的ROBU算法，利用低维变量块大幅减少所需子样本数，在高维和大样本场景下实现了可扩展的高效鲁棒估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的概率子抽样方法为获得不受异常值污染的初始回归估计量，需要考察的子集数量随预测变量数量指数增长，在中高维情形下不可行；近期提出的非随机策略在参数和观测数较大时也难以扩展。因此迫切需要一种可扩展的高效算法来为MM估计量提供好的初始点。

Method: 提出ROBU（Robust Orthogonal Block Updates）算法，核心思想是将解释变量划分为低维正交块，对每个块只需少量子样本即可高概率找到干净初始点，然后通过分块坐标下降方式迭代更新各块系数，最终得到全模型的鲁棒MM估计。

Result: 通过大量模拟实验和蛋白质组学数据应用，验证了ROBU算法所得估计量具有良好的鲁棒性，且与现有算法相比表现更优。

Conclusion: ROBU算法成功解决了MM估计量在高维大样本场景下初始点选取的计算瓶颈，是一种兼具鲁棒性和可扩展性的实用方法。

Abstract: Robust regression methods, particularly MM-estimators, are essential for analyzing datasets where heavy-tailed noise or high-leverage outliers may be present. Algorithms to compute these estimators are iterative and rely on having a good initial point. A widely-used probabilistic approach to obtaining an initial regression estimator that is not affected by outliers consists of fitting linear regression models to many random subsets of the training data. Unfortunately, the number of subsets that need to be considered in order to have a high probability of finding one that is clean of outliers grows exponentially with the number of predictors. This renders the approach unfeasible for models with a moderate to large numbers of variables. Alternative non-stochastic strategies that have been proposed recently also fail to scale well when the number of parameters and observations are large. To overcome this problem, we propose a highly scalable algorithm based on block-coordinate descent. Our Robust Orthogonal Block Updates (ROBU) algorithm uses lower-dimensional blocks of explanatory variables, which require much fewer sub-samples to find a good initial point with high probability. Extensive simulations and a proteogenomics data application illustrate the robustness properties of the estimators computed with ROBU and demonstrate that they compare favourably to those calculated with existing algorithms.

</details>


### [340] [$λ$-PSD: Scalable Approximate SNR-Optimised Polynomial Stein Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26621)
*Minh-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Long Vu,Christopher Drovandi,Leah F. South,Trung-Tin Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 多项式斯坦因差异（PSD）在提高多项式次数时，信噪比（SNR²）可能指数级衰减。为此，作者将斯坦因差异构造重新定义为显式的SNR²最大化问题，提出λ-PSD——一种低维子空间中的协方差感知重加权方案，能在保持线性时间复杂度的同时实现稳定的SNR²并显著提升检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PSD方法的统计性质理解不足，特别是增加多项式次数并非直接优化信噪比，反而可能导致SNR²随次数指数衰减，这一失效模式亟需解决。

Method: 作者首先分析了PSD中多项式次数与SNR的关系，揭示指数衰减问题。随后将斯坦因差异构造重新表述为显式的SNR²最大化问题，转化为斯坦因特征上的瑞利商。基于此提出λ-PSD：一种在低维子空间中近似的、可扩展的协方差感知重加权方案。

Result: 理论证明，在高斯设定下，λ-PSD避免了SNR²的指数崩溃，实现了稳定的SNR²。实验表明，λ-PSD大幅提升了检验功效，同时保持样本数量的线性时间复杂度。

Conclusion: 信噪比感知设计对可扩展斯坦因差异方法至关重要，λ-PSD通过显式优化SNR²，在保持计算效率的同时显著改善了统计性能。

Abstract: Polynomial Stein discrepancies (PSD) provide a scalable alternative to kernel Stein methods for measuring sample quality and goodness-of-fit testing, but their statistical properties remain poorly understood. We show that increasing polynomial degree primarily amplifies signal without adequately controlling variance, rather than directly optimising the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR).
